# 项目: recursive_gen_depth_est

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: gpu-6000pro-2
- SSH 连接命令: ssh connect.westb.seetacloud.com  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格

&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：分析 gpt2xl_topk50_001 实验结果

### 背景
gpt2xl_topk50_001 是 3×3 矩阵（3 模型 × 3 解码策略）的最后一格：GPT-2 XL + top-k k=50。实验已完成（cron 在 02:16 UTC 触发 on_complete），状态 ANALYZING，需要提取 metrics。

### 服务器信息
- 服务器: gpu-6000pro-2
- 工作目录: /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est
- Python: /root/miniconda3/bin/python
- 环境激活: source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base

### 你要做的事

1. **SSH 到 gpu-6000pro-2，读取结果文件**：
   - 先查看 results 目录结构: `ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/results/` 和 `ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/results/gpt2xl*/` 或类似路径
   - 找到 gpt2xl_topk50 相关的结果目录
   - 读取以下文件（如果存在）:
     - classification_report.txt（4-class 和 3-class）
     - pairwise_auc.json
     - feature_importance.json
     - three_class_* 相关文件
     - code_contamination 相关文件
     - finetune loss 日志

2. **也检查训练日志**：
   - 查看 tmux 日志或 nohup 输出: `ls -la /tmp/*gpt2xl*topk*` 或 `/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/logs/`
   - 查看 checkpoints: `ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/checkpoints/gpt2xl*topk*`

3. **提取完整 metrics 并更新 registry**：
   用 update_registry 更新 gpt2xl_topk50_001，设置：
   - status: DONE
   - conclusion: success 或 negative（基于 4-class acc 是否 > 40% 阈值）
   - metrics: 包含以下所有可用指标：
     - four_class: rf_accuracy, rf_accuracy_ci, lr_accuracy, lr_accuracy_ci
     - pairwise_auc: lr 和 rf 的 d0v1, d0v2, d0v3, d1v2, d1v3, d2v3
     - three_class: accuracy + ci
     - three_class_filtered: accuracy + ci + auc
     - feature_importance_top5
     - finetune_loss: 各 depth 的 loss
     - code_contamination: narrow_pct 和 broad_pct
   - notes: 包含与 GPT-2 XL nucleus baseline (83.3% RF) 和 temp09 (85.4% RF) 的对比表
   - spec: command, data_path, data_count 等
   - checkpoint_path 和 log_path

4. **与同模型其他策略对比**（写入 notes）：
   - GPT-2 XL nucleus: 83.3% 4-class RF, d1v2 AUC 0.913
   - GPT-2 XL temp09: 85.4% 4-class RF, d1v2 AUC 0.937
   - 参考 Pythia topk50: 69.6%（topk 在 Pythia 上降 8.6pp）
   - 参考 OLMo topk50: 75.7%（topk 在 OLMo 上降 3.1pp）

### 输出格式
完成 registry 更新后，输出一段简洁的分析总结（200字内），包含：
- 核心指标（4-class acc, d1v2 AUC, 3-class filtered acc）
- 与同模型 nucleus/temp09 的 delta
- top-k 对 GPT-2 XL 的影响幅度 vs Pythia/OLMo 的 top-k 影响
- 是否有意外发现

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have all the data. Let me also check if there's a three_class_filtered result since that directory was empty. Let me also check the features_filtered.jsonl file size to see if contamination filtering happened. Also, note that lora_depth_0 is a symlink to gpt2xl's checkpoint (shared depth_0 since it's the same model). Let me check how many lines are in the filtered data.

Let me now compile all metrics and update the registry.

Key metrics:
- 4-class RF: 74.45% (CI: 73.02-75.78%)


- 4-class LR: 71.30% (CI: 69.90-72.70%)
- 3-class RF: 69.47% (CI: 67.70-71.07%)
- 3-class LR: 65.07% (CI: 63.37-66.73%)

Looking at the pairwise AUC scores for both models across the 4-class setup, the logistic regression shows strong discrimination between most class pairs, particularly d0v3 at 0.9988, while random forest achieves 0.9906 on d0v1 with the remaining pairs showing varying levels of separation.

The 3-class AUC comparisons reveal that random forest outperforms logistic regression on the d1v2 and d2v3 comparisons, though both models struggle somewhat with the d2v3 distinction. Looking at feature importance, perplexity percentiles dominate the 4-class model's predictions, with p90_ppl accounting for nearly a quarter of the importance, followed by p75_ppl and mean surprisal. The finetuning losses show improvement from depth 1 to depth 2, and there's no contamination in the dataset.

Now I'm comparing these results against baseline models across different sampling strategies. The nucleus sampling baseline for GPT-2 XL achieves 83.3% accuracy on the 4-class task with random forest, but top-k sampling drops this to 74.45%, a loss of nearly 9 percentage points. The temperature 0.9 variant shows an even larger gap of about 11 points. The AUC metrics tell a similar story—nucleus sampling performs substantially better than top-k across the d1v2 comparison. When I look at cross-model performance, both Pythia and OLMo show consistent degradation with top-k sampling compared to nucleus, suggesting this is a general pattern rather than model-specific.

The key finding is that OLMo's top-k performance of 75.7% still clears the 40% success threshold, so despite the performance drop, the approach is working.

Now I'm updating the registry to capture all these metrics.
```

### Assistant

_source line 87 · 2026-05-15T02:44:44.103Z_

Registry 已更新为 DONE。分析总结：

**GPT-2 XL top-k k=50**: 4-class RF **74.45%** (CI 73.0-75.8%), d1v2 AUC **0.813**, 3-class RF **69.47%**。

与同模型对比：相比 nucleus baseline (83.3%) 下降 **-8.85pp**，相比 temp09 (85.4%) 下降 **-10.95pp**。d1v2 AUC 从 0.913/0.937 降至 0.813，中间深度区分力显著削弱。

跨模型 top-k 影响：GPT-2 XL 降幅 (-8.85pp) 与 Pythia (-8.6pp) 几乎一致，OLMo 仅降 3.1pp。Top-k 截断对 GPT-2 XL 和 Pythia 的深度信号破坏程度相当，OLMo 更鲁棒。

值得注意：尽管 top-k 是三种策略中最差的，74.45% 仍是随机基线 (25%) 的 ~3 倍，深度信号在 top-k 下依然存在且可检测。three_class_filtered 结果缺失，contamination filtering 未跑完。
